Executive summary

DocuLens found 44 assessable management guidance items and resolved 17 against a comparable later actual (10 in the active cohorts). 14 items remain open: 3 missing target-period coverage, 11 without a stated deadline, and 0 not yet due. 13 comparisons have a scored outcome. 11 items are closed as Not Verifiable After Exhaustive Search where the loaded evidence did not prove a comparable outcome; they are not treated as misses. 20 explicit-target item(s) are shown in the appendix outside the active cohorts.
